## Analyzer Architecture Summary

kserve-autogluon-server is a Kubernetes component. managing 12 CRD(s)\: ClusterStorageContainer, LLMInferenceServiceConfig, LLMInferenceService, LocalModelCache, LocalModelNamespaceCache, LocalModelNodeGroup, LocalModelNode, ClusterServingRuntime, InferenceGraph, InferenceService, ServingRuntime, TrainedModel. deploying 3 workload(s) (kserve-controller-manager, llmisvc-controller-manager, kserve-localmodel-controller-manager) with 4 container(s). watching 32 resource type(s). exposing 6 service(s) on ports\: 443, 8443. with 24 webhook(s) (15 validating, 5 mutating). connecting to external services\: azure-blob, gcs, httpx, requests, s3, weaviate. runtime dependencies\: knative-local-gateway. defining 47 API spec type(s) across 90 struct(s) with 303 total fields. with 1162 operator config constants (3 image references, 13 env vars). following 12 reconciliation steps across 9 controller(s). reporting 34 status condition types. with 3 ingress route(s). Python k8s client with 68 API call(s). integrates with Istio, KServe, Prometheus, management relationship via labels/annotations. deploys kube-rbac-proxy sidecar for RBAC-based authorization. kustomize overlays\: config/base, config/certmanager/kserve, config/certmanager, config/certmanager/llmisvc, config/certmanager/localmodel, config/clusterresources, config/components/kserve, config/components/llmisvc, config/components/localmodel, config/configmap, config/crd/full/clusterstoragecontainer, config/crd/full, config/crd/full/llmisvc, config/crd/full/localmodel, config/crd, config/crd/minimal/clusterstoragecontainer, config/crd/minimal, config/crd/minimal/llmisvc, config/crd/minimal/localmodel, config/default, config/llmisvc, config/llmisvcconfig, config/localmodelnodes, config/localmodels, config/manager, config/overlays/addons/kserve, config/overlays/addons/llmisvc, config/overlays/addons/localmodel, config/overlays/all, config/overlays/dev-image-config, config/overlays/development, config/overlays/kubeflow, config/overlays/llmisvc, config/overlays/standalone/kserve, config/overlays/standalone/llmisvc, config/overlays/test-llmisvc, config/overlays/test-modelcache, config/overlays/version-template/cluster-resources, config/overlays/version-template, config/rbac, config/rbac/llmisvc, config/rbac/localmodel, config/rbac/localmodelnode, config/runtimes, config/storagecontainers, config/webhook, config/webhook/llmisvc, config/webhook/localmodel. references components\: kserve (client, import, provider), vllm (import).
